# Agents · W · part 2

900 entries, web-footprint-scan to weekly-synthesis. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **web-footprint-scan**: Sweeps the target's external web footprint — news mentions, press releases, directory listings, manufacturer dealer locators, trade-association memberships, customer/GC references, public records cues — and tests for PE-ownership signals on a labeled confidence ladder. Wave R1 of the recon pipeline. Read-only; budget-capped; touches untrusted external web content. ([ZaBrisket/vortex-tuck-in-analyst](https://github.com/ZaBrisket/vortex-tuck-in-analyst/blob/HEAD/plugins/vortex-tuck-in-analyst/agents/web-footprint-scan.md))
